- **.NET Architect**: ASP.NET Core, Blazor, EF Core — Architektur-Entscheidungen, Refactoring-Pläne, Tech-Stack-Abwägungen. Gibt strukturierte Empfehlungen ohne Code zu schreiben. USE FOR: Architektur-Entscheidungen, Solution Design, .NET-Technologieauswahl. DO NOT USE FOR: Code schreiben (use dotnet-developer), Tests planen (use tunit-tester). ([GrexyLoco/K.Agents](https://github.com/GrexyLoco/K.Agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/kagents/agents/dotnet-architect.agent.md))
- **.NET Developer**: C# 14 / .NET 10 development — Blazor components, MAUI views, ASP.NET Core APIs, services, DTOs, dependency injection, middleware. USE FOR: writing, refactoring, and debugging .NET code across Blazor, MAUI, and ASP.NET Core. DO NOT USE FOR: database schema design (use database-engineer), architecture decisions (use app-architect), or writing tests (use tunit-tester). ([GrexyLoco/K.Agents](https://github.com/GrexyLoco/K.Agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/kagents/agents/dotnet-developer.agent.md))
- **.NET Expert**: ASP.NET Core implementation authority. Owns production-grade C#/.NET services, Clean/Vertical Slice architecture implementation, API handlers, EF Core usage, validation, performance, logging, and backend test quality. Use when ASP.NET Core APIs, C#/.NET services, EF Core data access, MediatR/CQRS handlers, background jobs, .NET auth integration, or NuGet dependency and target-framework decisions are in scope, or a review finding flags backend correctness, async misuse, or performance in .NET code. ([zulfahmi93/my-workflow](https://github.com/zulfahmi93/my-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dotnet-expert.md) · ★1)

- **2026-11-03-fixes-guard-all-outgoing-refs**: The pre-push reader overwrote its candidate for every stdin row, then judged only the final ref. A batch could therefore hide an unrecorded branch behind a recorded sibling, or reject a valid batch when an already-public ancestor alias happened to come last. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/2026-11-03-fixes-guard-all-outgoing-refs.md))
- **2026-11-03-groa-no-longer-hangs-on-the-hound**: `bin/agents/groa-update.sh` now bounds and detaches the two maintenance calls it makes after the fast-forward — `bin/ward/garmr.sh refresh` and `groa-agent/seat.sh --quiet`. Each runs under `setsid timeout -k 2` with its own stdin and output closed, so a refresh that waits on a console, a polkit prompt or a missing unit can no longer stall the whole update. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/2026-11-03-groa-no-longer-hangs-on-the-hound.md))
